<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What percentage of Mark's counters are blue?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 100} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 25 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 25 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 25 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$25 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 100} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 25 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 25 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 25 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$25 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Mark has 40 counters in his bag.

How many of his counters are not blue?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Green,
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 40 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
&= 20
\end{aligned}
$

Red,
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 40 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 10
\end{aligned}
$

Not blue,
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20 + 10 \\\\
&= 30
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$30$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Green,
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 40 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
&= 20
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Red,
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 40 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 10
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Not blue,
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20 + 10 \\\\
&= 30
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$30$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subsubquestion'>
<div class='question subsubquestion'>

It takes $13$ oranges to make $1 \ \text{litre}$ of juice.

How many oranges are needed to make $25 \ \text{litres}$ of juice?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Oranges needed} &= 13 \times 25 \\\\
                       &= 325 \ \text{oranges}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$325 \ \text{oranges}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Oranges needed} &= 13 \times 25 \\\\
                       &= 325 \ \text{oranges}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$325 \ \text{oranges}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

This is a square-based pyramid

It has $5$ faces, which are a square and four congruent isosceles triangles.

In the space below, draw an accurate net for this pyramid.
